Implement _multiplicative_power(p0, rng, time, q_start=2, q_end=128):
Pseudo:
```
def _multiplicative_power(p0, rng, time_limit, q_start=2.0, q_end=128.0, verbose=False):
    p=sanitize floor
    best=p; bestsc=score(p)
    q=q_start; alpha=0.5; stagnant=0
    deadline=...
    while time<deadline:
        c=conv(p,p)
        M=max(c)
        if M<=0: break
        r=c/M
        # weights = r**q ; if q small; normalize
        if q<1: w=r
        else: w=r**q ; w /= max(sumw,1)
        h=correlate_w_p(w,p) # valid front
        maxh=max(h)
        if maxh<=1e-14: perturb
        else: h/=maxh
        # candidates for alpha
        base_score=bestsc? cur_score maybe exact from c*2m
        cur_score=2*m*M
        alphas=[alpha*0.35, alpha*0.7, alpha, alpha*2, alpha*4] within [1e-4,50]
        choose best candidate by metric = score_candidate + 1e-5* maybe lq? Actually exact score enough.
        But line search 5 * each expensive (conv). We can reduce to 3: [alpha*0.5, alpha, alpha*2].
        if improvement > 1e-10: accept, alpha=chosen, stagn=0; update best if better
        else: alpha*=0.6; stagn++
        if stagn>20: perturb p; reset q=q_start maybe? 
        # gradually increase q: every 20 iterations q=min(q_end, q*1.1)
```

But each candidate exact score requires full conv. We already computed c for direction, but candidates need new c. Could use approximate gradient for multiple α: for small α, score decrease approx α∑ p_i h_i? But line search exact maybe okay. For n=4096 and 500 candidates/s? Maybe line search 3*500=1500/s; 2400s =3.6M. Fine.

Need compute h efficiently: h_i = ∑_j p_j w_{i+j}. This is cross-correlation of w and p. Implement _w_p_cross(w,p):
We need length n output. w length 2n-1. Could use FFT. Let's define:
```
def _cross_w_p(w, p):
    n=len(p); L=next_pow2(max(len(w),2*n))
    fw=fft(w,L); fp=fft(p,L)
    out=irfft(fw * conjugate(fp), L)[:n]
    # valid? Need verify orientation: For w[0..2n-3], p[0..n-1], desired h[i]=∑_{j=0}^{n-1} p[j] w[i+j]. Circular corr with p? Let out[k]=∑_j w[k+j mod L] p[j]? Detailed helper functions:
```

```
def _pw2(x):
    L=1
    x=int(max(1,x))
    while L<x: L*=2
    return L
```

```
def _convolve_ab(a,b):
    la, lb = len(a), len(b)
    if la==0 or lb==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if la*lb <= 200000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    L=_pw2(la+lb-1)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    if b is a:
        fb=fa
    else: fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)
    return out[:la+lb-1].astype(float, copy=False)
```
Need avoid `b is a` comparison works. If square, can do `fa*fa`.

```
def _conv_square(p):
    n=len(p)
    if n<2: return p*p? np.convolve? For n==1: return [p[0]^2]
    L=_pw2(2*n-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,L)
    out[out<0 & out> -1e-15]=0
    return out[:2*n-1]
```
For small n, np.convolve. Use direct for n<=256 to reduce overhead? FFT ok. But for n=128, FFT overhead maybe. We'll threshold: if n <= 256: np.convolve. Since many small scores. Good.

```
def _correlate_w_p(w,p):
    n=len(p); m=len(w)
    if n==0: return array
    if n < 256 and m < 256: direct? Not needed.
    L=_pw2(max(m, 2*n))
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp),L)
    res=out[:n]
    return np.where(res>0, res, 0.0) # Should be nonnegative? w,p nonneg; but numeric.
```
Need verify orientation with direct test small? Could be `out = ifft(fw * conj(fp))` returns h0=∑ w[0?]*p[0]? Let's test mentally with p=[1,2], w=[a,b,c]. Frequency: (W * conj(P)) inverse? DFT: If defined DFT X_k=∑x_n e^{-i2πkn/N}, inverse 1/N∑X_k e^{i2πkn/N}. Correlation r[k]=∑_n x[n+k] y[n] has DFT X_k Y_k^* (if x, y with appropriate). So iffft(W * conj(P))[k]=∑_n w[k+n mod L] p[n]. Score function:
```
def _score_normalized(p):
   p=_sanitize_vec(p)?
   n=len(p); if n==0 return inf
   c=_conv_square(p)
   if c.size==0: return inf, 0
   maxc=float(np.max(c))
   ssq=float(np.dot(c,c))
   score=2.0*n*maxc
   return score, ssq
```

Sanitize:
```
def _sanitize(p):
    arr=np.asarray(p,dtype=float).ravel()
    if arr.size==0: return None
    arr=np.where(np.isfinite(arr), arr, 0.0)
    arr=arr[arr>0]? But if some zero? np.clip(arr,0,None)
    arr=np.clip(arr,0.0,1000.0)
    s=arr.sum()
    if not finite or s<=1e-18: return None
    return arr/s
```
For optimization, floor after sanitize: `p=np.maximum(p, floor); p/=p.sum()`.

Resampling:
```
def _resample(a,m):
    a=_sanitize(a); if None return None
    n=len(a)
    if n==m: return a.copy()
    x_old=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
    x_new=(np.arange(m)+0.5)/m
    b=np.interp(x_new,x_old,a)
    b=np.maximum(b,0.0)
    return _sanitize(b)
```
If b all zero? not.

Smoothing by Gaussian maybe for starts:
```
def _gauss_smooth(p, sigma):
   n=len(p); if n<5 or sigma<=0 return p
   rad=int(3*sigma)+2
   k=np.arange(-rad,rad+1, float)
   kern=np.exp(-0.5*(k/sigma)**2); kern/=kern.sum()
   out=np.convolve(p,kern,mode='same')
   return _sanitize(out)
```

```
def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=None):
  if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,10))
  t=(np.arange(n)+0.5)/n
  if mode==0: z=1; for k 1..8: amp=rng.normal(0,1/(k+1)); ph=rng.uniform(0,2π); z+=amp*np.cos(2πkt+ph); p=np.log1p(exp(clip(z)))
  elif 1: a=uniform(0.05,3); b=... p=t^a*(1-t)^b
  elif 2: K bumps
  elif 3: spline
  elif 4: blocks
  elif 5: logistic+gauss
  elif 6: sine series: z=1 + sum amp_k sin(2πk t + ph) with amp random 0.1/k? clip z>0; p=z
  elif 7: gamma-like: p=np.power(np.sin(np.pi*t), alpha) * np.power? or abs(sin(2πt+phase))^p
  elif 8: ramp + sine: beta=uniform(-1,1)*0.9; A=uniform(-0.8,0.8); phase; z=1+beta*(2t-1)+A*np.sin(2πt+phase); p=np.maximum(z,0)
  elif 9: three or four blocks with random edges and heights (including asymmetric)
  else: random positive noise (low-pass) p=1+noise smoothed
  p=np.nanmax... /n? Actually p values not normalized; _sanitize.
  return _sanitize(p + 1e-10)
```

```
def _smooth_descent(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=20, beta_end=2000):
```

```
def _power_mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, q_start=2.0, q_end=64.0, alpha=None, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize_vec(p0); if None return None, inf
    if time_limit<=0: return p,_exact_score(p)
    m=p.size
    floor=1e-18/m
    p=np.maximum(p,floor); p/=p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_score=_exact_score(p); best_ss=...
    cur_score=best_score; cur_ss=...
    q=float(max(0.5,q_start)); qmax=float(max(q,q_end))
    alpha=0.5 if alpha is None else alpha
    stall=0; it=0; last_print=-10000
    deadline=time.monotonic()+time_limit
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
       c=_conv_full(p,p)
       c=np.where(finite,c,0); c=np.maximum(c,0)
       maxc=float(np.max(c))
       if maxc<=1e-300: break
       cur_score=2*m*maxc
       cur_ss=float(np.dot(c,c))
       if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12 or (abs...)?? update best (but p may not be best? only line search produced improvements)
       # weights
       r=c/maxc
       if q <=1.0: w=r.copy()
       else: w=np.power(r,q) # could produce nan if r negative no
       wsum=float(w.sum())
       if not finite wsum or wsum<=1e-300: w=np.full(len(c),1/len(c)); wsum=1
       else: w/=wsum
       h=_conv_valid_front(w,p)
       h=np.where(finite,h,0); h=np.maximum(h,0)
       maxh=float(np.max(h))
       if maxh <=1e-15:
           # perturb
       else:
           h=h/maxh
           tests = []
           for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0): a=alpha*mult; if 1e-3<=a<=50: tests.append(a)
           if not tests: tests=[0.1]
           chosen=None; chosen_score=cur_score; chosen_ss=cur_ss; chosen_a=alpha
           for a in tests:
              cand = p*np.exp(-a*h)
              cand = np.maximum(cand,floor)
              s=cand.sum(); ...
              sc, ss = _score_normalized(cand)? But _exact_score only score. Need ss. Use local `_score_and_ss(cand)`.
              if better_than(sc,ss, chosen_score,chosen_ss): choose
           if chosen is not None:
              p=chosen; cur_score=chosen_score; cur_ss=chosen_ss; alpha=chosen_a/(mult? Actually chosen_a actual)
              stall=0
              if improved best update
              # occasional pull back if drift: if cur_score > best_score*1.15 and rng<0.2: p=best
              # adapt alpha: if chosen_a==tests[-1]? increase? We set alpha=chosen_a * 0.7? Since if best at 4, maybe keep high. We'll set alpha = chosen_a * 0.8 if not max? Simpler alpha=chosen_a.
           else:
              stall+=1; alpha*=0.6
              if stall>=12: perturb etc.
              if stall>60: reset to best, q=q_start, alpha=0.5, stall=0
       it+=1
       if it%20==0: q=min(qmax, q*1.12)
       if verbose and ... print
    return best, best_score
```

```
def _is_better(sc, ss, best_sc, best_ss):
   if sc < best_sc - 1e-10: return True
   if abs(sc - best_sc) <= 1e-9:
       return ss < best_ss * (1 - 1e-9) - 1e-16
   return False
```

```
def _score_ss_arr(a):
    v=_sanitize_vec(a)
    if v is None: return (inf, inf)
    n=v.size
    c=_conv_full(v,v)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    if c.size==0: return inf, inf
    maxc=float(np.max(c))
    if not finite maxc: return inf, inf
    ss=float(np.dot(c,c))
    score=2.0*n*maxc
    return (float(score), float(ss))
```

Implementation of modes:
```
def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=None):
    if n<=0: return None
    if mode is None: mode=int(rng.integers(0,10))
    t=(np.arange(n,float)+0.5)/float(n)
    if mode==0:
      z=1.0
      for k in range(1,10):
        amp=float(rng.normal(0,1/(k+1)))
        ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
        z = z + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*k*t + ph)
      p=np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z,-50,50)))
    elif mode==1:
      a=uniform(0.05,3); b=...
      tt=clip(t,1e-8,1-1e-8)
      p=tt**a * (1-tt)**b
    elif mode==2: Gaussian bumps
    elif mode==3: spline
    elif mode==4: blocks
    elif mode==5: logistic/hybrid
    elif mode==6:
      z=1.0
      for k in range(1,7):
        amp=float(rng.uniform(0.05,0.9/(k+0.5))) * (1 if rng.random()<0.5 else -1?) maybe positive amplitude with phase.
        ph=float(rng.uniform(0,2np.pi))
        z += amp*np.sin(2np.pi*k*t + ph)
      p=z
    elif mode==7:
      a=uniform(0.3,4); ph=uniform(0,2π); p=np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*t + ph?))? Need nonnegative. Use p=np.power(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi*t + ph)), a) + 1e-6
    elif mode==8:
      beta=uniform(-0.9,0.9); A=uniform(-0.8,0.8); ph=uniform(0,2π)
      z=1 + beta*(2*t-1) + A*np.sin(2π t + ph)
      p=np.maximum(z,0)
    elif mode==9:
      # random multi-block asymmetric, K 4-12, plus optional linear trend
      K=int(rng.integers(4,12))
      edges sorted; heights lognormal; random endpoint scaling; p piecewise + optional trend
    else:
      # lowpass random noise (+ cosine)
      z=1.0
      for k in range(1,6): z += rng.normal(0,0.5/(k+1))*np.cos(...)
      p=np.maximum(np.exp(np.clip(z, -3, 3)), 1e-6)
    p = np.where(finite, p, 0); p=np.clip(p,0,1000)
    if p.size and max(p)>0 maybe; p +=1e-10
    s=p.sum(); if s<=0 p=ones
    return p/s
```
For mode 9, implement piecewise with edges:

```
K = int(rng.integers(3, 12))
edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K))
blocks = np.concatenate(([0],edges,[1]))
heights = rng.lognormal(0,1,K+1)
p = np.zeros(n)
for ii in range(K+1): s=max(0,int(blocks[ii]*n)); e=max(s+1,int(blocks[ii+1]*n)); p[s:e]=heights[ii]
opt = rng.uniform(0,2π)
```

Power descent function code in detail:
```
def _power_mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, q_start=2.0, q_end=64.0, alpha_init=0.5, verbose=False):
    p=_sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    if time_limit<=0:
       sc,_=_score_ss_arr(p); return p, sc
    m=int(p.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / float(m)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()
    best = p.copy()
    best_score, best_ss = _score_ss_arr(best)
    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
    q = float(max(0.5, q_start))
    q_max = float(max(q, q_end))
    alpha = float(max(1e-3, alpha_init))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stall=0; it=0; last_print=-10000
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_full(p,p)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if maxc <= 0.0:
            break
        cur_score = 2.0 * m * maxc
        cur_ss = float(np.dot(c,c))
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11 or (abs(cur_score-best_score)<=1e-10 and cur_ss < best_ss):
            best = p.copy(); best_score=cur_score; best_ss=cur_ss
        # weights
        r = c / maxc
        if q <= 1.0:
            w = r.copy()
        else:
            # use power, with possible overflow? r<=1, no.
            w = np.power(r, q)
        wsum = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 1e-300:
            inv_len = 1.0 / float(len(c))
            w = np.full(len(c), inv_len, dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / wsum
            wsum=1.0
        # small uniform regularization for weights
        w = 0.9995 * w + 0.0005 * (1.0 / float(len(c)))
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum > 0: w /= wsum
        h = _conv_valid_front(w,p)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
        if maxh <= 1e-18:
            # perturbation
        else:
            h = h / maxh
            tests=[]
            for mult in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0):
                a=alpha*mult
                if 1e-3 <= a <= 60.0:
                    tests.append(float(a))
            if not tests:
                tests=[0.05,0.2,1.0]
            chosen=None; chosen_score=cur_score; chosen_ss=cur_ss; chosen_a=alpha
            for a in tests:
                dec = np.exp(-a * h)
                cand = p * dec
                cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
                s=float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 0 or not finite: continue
                cand = cand / s
                sc, ss = _score_ss_arr(cand)
                better=False
                if not finite sc: continue
                if sc < chosen_score - 1e-11:
                    better=True
                elif abs(sc - chosen_score) <= 1e-9:
                    if ss < chosen_ss * (1.0 - 1e-8) - 1e-16:
                        better=True
                if better:
                    chosen=cand; chosen_score=sc; chosen_ss=ss; chosen_a=a
            if chosen is not None:
                p=chosen; cur_score=chosen_score; cur_ss=chosen_ss; alpha=chosen_a
                stall=0
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11 or (...): update best
                # if drift
                if cur_score > best_score * 1.10 and rng.random() < 0.2:
                   p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score; cur_ss=best_ss
            else:
                stall +=1
                alpha = max(1e-3, alpha*0.65)
                if stall >= 15:
                   # perturb
                   std = 0.05 if stall<50 else min(0.45, 0.05+0.004*stall)
                   p_try = p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,std,m))
                   p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor); p_try/=p_try.sum()
                   sc_try, ss_try = _score_ss_arr(p_try)
                   if finite and sc_try <= max(best_score*1.08, cur_score*1.05):
                       p=p_try; cur_score=sc_try; cur_ss=ss_try
                   else:
                       p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score; cur_ss=best_ss
                   # smoothing try maybe
                   if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                       p_smooth = _smooth_array(p, samples=1.0)
                       if p_smooth is not None:
                           sc_s, ss_s = _score_ss_arr(p_smooth)
                           if sc_s < cur_score - 1e-10:
                               p=p_smooth; cur_score=sc_s; cur_ss=ss_s
                   stall=0
                if stall > 70:
                   p=best.copy(); cur_score=best_score; cur_ss=best_ss; alpha=0.5; q=max(1.0, q*0.5); stall=0
        it +=1
        if it % 15 == 0:
            q = min(q_max, q * 1.10)
        if verbose and (it-last_print>=300):
            print("power m n... best")
    return best, best_score
```

### Helper details

`_sanitize_vec(p)`:
```
def _sanitize_vec(p):
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim !=1 or a.size==0: return None
    a = a.copy()
    a[~np.isfinite(a)] = 0.0
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300: return None
    return a / s
```
No boolean concerns? If input list of int/float, fine.

```
def _score_only(a): return _score_ss_arr(a)[0]
```

`_conv_full(a,b)`: Need if `b is a` for square. But because arrays can be views, use `if a is b` or maybe pass same? In `_score_ss` call `_conv_full(v, v)`, same object. Good.
```
def _conv_full(a,b):
    la=int(a.size); lb=int(b.size)
    if la==0 or lb==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    out=la+lb-1
    if la*lb <= 2000000:
       return np.asarray(np.convolve(a,b), dtype=float)
    L=_next_pow2(out)
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L)
    if a is b:
       out_fft=fa*fa
    else:
       fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
       out_fft=fa*fb
    res=np.fft.irfft(out_fft,L)
    return res[:out].astype(float, copy=False)
```
If direct for small, `np.convolve` may return slightly negative? fine.

`_conv_valid_front(w,p)` from previous:
```
def _conv_valid_front(w,p):
    lenw=int(w.size); lenp=int(p.size)
    if lenp==0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    if lenw*lenp <= 2000000:
       # Direct for small: valid? Need sum_i p[i]*w[i+j]? Could implement loop or fft. For small, FFT ok.
    L=_next_pow2(max(lenw, 2*lenp))
    fw=np.fft.rfft(w,L)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    out=np.fft.irfft(fw*np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:lenp].astype(float, copy=False)
```
Use always FFT. lenw maybe 1? fine. Need if lenw=0? Not from score. If n=1, L=next_pow2(max(1,2))=2, ok.

```
def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    budget=float(budget_s)
    if not finite or budget<=30: budget=60
    start=time.monotonic()
    deadline=start+max(60,budget-30)
    try: iseed=int(seed)
    except: iseed=12345
    rng=np.random.default_rng((iseed%1000000007)+1)
    cands=[]
    parent_arr=None
    try: raw=height_sequence_1
    except: raw=None
    if raw is not None:
       try: parent_arr=_sanitize_vec(np.asarray(raw,dtype=float))
       except: parent_arr=None
    if parent_arr is not None:
       sc=_score_only(parent_arr); _add_candidate(cands,parent_arr,sc,16)
    # Fallback if no parent and little time
    fallback_n=2048
    # Small exploration
    rem=deadline-time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260:
       small_n=1024
       small_starts=[]
       tb=_two_block_best(small_n); if tb: small_starts.append(_smooth_array(tb,0.5) or tb)
       if parent_arr is not None: sp=_resample(parent_arr,small_n); small_starts.append(_smooth_array(sp,0.5) or sp)
       # add a few initial modes: 8,6,4,0,1,9
       for mode in (8,6,4,0,1,9,7):
           small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, mode))
       # limit to 6 maybe

          rem=deadline-now; if rem<40: break
          t=min(25,max(5,rem-120))
          p,sc=_power_mpd(sp, rng, t, q_start=2.0, q_end=16.0, alpha_init=0.7)
          _add_candidate(cands,p,sc,8)
          if best in cands < target? not needed
    # determine final_n
    rem = deadline-now
    if rem > 900: final_n=6144
    elif rem > 450: final_n=4096
    else: final_n=2048
    if rem <= 100:
       best candidate returned.
    # Build final starts
    final_starts=[]
    tb=_two_block_best(final_n); if tb: final_starts.append(_smooth_array(tb,1.0) or tb)
    if parent_arr is not None:
       sp=_resample(parent_arr, final_n); sp=_smooth_array(sp,0.75); sp=_sanitize_vec(sp*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.04,final_n))); if sp: final_starts.append(sp)
    sorted_cands = sorted(cands, key=_cand_key)
    for item in sorted_cands[:3]:
       arr=item[2]
       if arr is None: continue
       sp=_resample(arr, final_n)
       if sp is not None:
          sp=_sanitize_vec(sp*np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.03,final_n)))
          final_starts.append(sp)
    # modes maybe (0,2,4,6,8,9,1,7,3,5)
    for mode in (8,6,4,0,1,9,7,2,3,5):
       final_starts.append(_initial_sequence(final_n, rng, mode))
    # score starts, top 5
    scored=[]
    for sp in final_starts:
       if sp is not None:
          sc,ss=_score_ss_arr(sp)
          if finite: scored.append((sc,ss,sp))
    # sort by score then ss
    scored.sort(key=_start_key) # define key returning (score,ss)? Python sort key can tuple. function `_start_key(item): return (item[0], item[1])`.

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```

```
def _score_ss_fast(a):
    aa=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
    if aa.size==0: return inf,inf
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(aa)): aa=np.where(np.isfinite(aa),aa,0)
    aa=np.maximum(aa,0)
    s=aa.sum()
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf,inf
    if not np.isfinite(s): return inf,inf
    if s != 1.0: aa = aa / s
    n=aa.size
    c=_conv_full(aa,aa)
    c=np.where(np.isfinite(c),c,0.0); c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    if c.size==0: return inf,inf
    maxc=float(np.max(c)); if not finite: return inf,inf
    ssq=float(np.dot(c,c))
    return (2.0*n*maxc, ssq)
```

Let's write `_score_ss_fast` carefully:
```
def _score_ss_fast(a):
    aa = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if aa.ndim != 1: aa = aa.ravel()
    if aa.size == 0: return (inf, inf)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(aa)):
        aa = np.where(np.isfinite(aa), aa, 0.0)
    aa = np.maximum(aa, 0.0)
    s = float(aa.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s): return (inf, inf)
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        aa = aa / s
    n = int(aa.size)
    c = _conv_full(aa, aa)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0: return (inf, inf)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(maxc) or maxc <= 0.0: return (float('inf'), 0.0)
    ss = float(np.dot(c, c))
    if not np.isfinite(ss): ss = 0.0
    return (float(2.0 * n * maxc), float(ss))
```

```
def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit):
   p=_sanitize_vec; floor; best; best_score=...
   factors=(0.45,0.7,0.9,1.1,1.4,2.0)
   deadline...
   stagnant=0
   while time<deadline:
       choose width w
       start
       base=p.copy(); current=p; cur_score,cur_ss=...
       for f in factors:
          cand=base.copy(); cand[start:start+w]*=f; sanitize+floor+normalize; sc,ss=_score_ss_fast(cand); if better than current accept
       if changed: p=current; update best if sc<best; stagnant=0
       else: stagnant++; if stagnant>50: perturbation etc; if stagnant>300 reset best
   return best,best_score
```

### Verify `_conv_valid_front` orientation with direct small

```
    e = max(s+1, min(n, int(round(edges[ii+1]*n))))
```

```
def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=1500.0, alpha_init=0.2, verbose=False):
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None: return None, inf
    if time_limit <=0: sc,_=_score_ss_fast(p); return p,sc
    m = int(p.size)
    floor=1e-18/m
    p=np.maximum(p,floor); p=p/p.sum()
    best=p.copy(); best_exact, _ = _score_ss_fast(best)
    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, float(beta_start))
    beta = max(1.0,float(beta_start)); beta_max=max(beta,float(beta_end))
    alpha = float(alpha_init)
    deadline = time.monotonic()+float(time_limit)
    stall=0; it=0; last_print=-10000
    while time.monotonic()<deadline:
        cur_exact, cur_smooth, h, maxh = _gradient_metrics(p,beta)
        if not finite maxh or maxh <=1e-15:
            p_try=...
            if p_try not None:
                e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try,beta)
                if finite e and e <= best_exact*1.05:
                    p=p_try; cur Exact...
            stall +=1
        else:
            h_h = h/np.max(h)
            tests=[]; for mult... (0.5,1,2,4); a=alpha*mult; range 1e-4,20
            chosen=None; ch_smooth=cur_smooth; ch_exact=cur_exact; ch_a=alpha
            for a in tests:
                dec=np.exp(-a*h_h); cand=p*dec; max floor; s; norm
                e,s2=_exact_smooth(cand,beta)
                if finite e and finite s2 and s2 + 1e-12*max(1,ch_smooth) < ch_smooth:
                    chosen...
            if chosen is not None:
                p=chosen; cur_smooth=ch_smooth; cur_exact=ch_exact; alpha=ch_a; stall=0
                if cur_exact < best_exact: best...
                if cur_exact > best_exact*1.25 and rng.random()<0.25: reset best; cur_exact,cur_smooth=_exact_smooth(p,beta)
            else:
                stall+=1; alpha=max(1e-4,alpha*0.6)
                if stall>=12: perturb; accept if e <= best*1.05 else reset; stall=0
                if stall>80: reset best; beta=max(beta,beta_start); alpha=alpha_init?; stall=0
        it+=1
        if it%20==0: beta=min(beta_max,beta*1.15)
        if verbose... print
    return best,best_exact
```

```python
"""
Search for a non-negative coefficient sequence minimizing

    2*n*max(conv(a,a)) / sum(a)**2

by combining:
  - multiplicative power-norm/smax descent on the probability simplex,
  - smoothed-max descent for polishing,
  - structured multi-resolution starts (block, Gaussian, spline, Fourier,
    ramp + sine, multi-block, random low-pass),
  - contour-block local scaling,
  - resampling to longer lengths,
  - small official evaluator checks at the end.

The returned vector is normalized to sum 1, which leaves the objective
unchanged.
"""

import numpy as np
import time


def _next_pow2(x):
    """Smallest power of two >= x."""
    x = int(max(1, x))
    L = 1
    while L < x:
        L <<= 1
    return L


def _conv_full(a, b):
    """Full real convolution.  Uses FFT for larger products."""
    la = int(a.size)
    lb = int(b.size)
    if la == 0 or lb == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    out_len = la + lb - 1
    if la * lb <= 250000:
        return np.asarray(np.convolve(a, b), dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(out_len)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
    if a is b:
        out_fft = fa * fa
    else:
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
        out_fft = fa * fb
    out = np.fft.irfft(out_fft, L)
    return out[:out_len].astype(float, copy=False)


def _conv_valid_front(w, p):
    """
    Return v[j] = sum_i p[i] * w[j+i], j=0..len(p)-1.

    This is the cross-correlation form needed for gradients w.r.t. p.
    """
    lenw = int(w.size)
    lenp = int(p.size)
    if lenp == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    L = _next_pow2(max(lenw, 2 * lenp))
    fw = np.fft.rfft(w, L)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    out = np.fft.irfft(fw * np.conjugate(fp), L)
    return out[:lenp].astype(float, copy=False)


def _sanitize_vec(p):
    """Clip to finite non-negative and normalize to sum 1."""
    a = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    if a.ndim != 1 or a.size == 0:
        return None
    a = a.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return None
    return a / s


def _score_ss_fast(a):
    """
    Score 2*n*max(conv(a,a))/sum(a)**2 plus sum of squares of conv.
    Works on non-normalized arrays and is intended for already-valid vectors.
    """
    aa = np.asarray(a, dtype=float)
    if aa.ndim != 1:
        aa = aa.ravel()
    if aa.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(aa)):
        aa = np.where(np.isfinite(aa), aa, 0.0)
    aa = np.maximum(aa, 0.0)
    s = float(aa.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    if abs(s - 1.0) > 1e-12:
        aa = aa / s
    n = int(aa.size)
    c = _conv_full(aa, aa)
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return float("inf"), 0.0
    ss = float(np.dot(c, c))
    if not np.isfinite(ss):
        ss = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * maxc), float(ss)


def _score_only(a):
    """Official objective value for a valid non-negative array."""
    return _score_ss_fast(a)[0]


def _cand_key(item):
    return item[0]


def _start_key(item):
    return (item[0], item[1])


def _prune_candidates(cands, keep):
    if len(cands) <= keep:
        return cands
    cands.sort(key=_cand_key)
    del cands[keep:]
    return cands


def _add_candidate(cands, arr, score, keep=16):
    a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
    if a is None:
        return
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        score, _ = _score_ss_fast(a)
    cands.append((float(score), int(a.size), a.copy()))
    _prune_candidates(cands, keep)


def _smooth_array(p, samples=1.0):
    """Mild positive Gaussian smoothing, keeping sum 1."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return None
    s = float(samples)
    if s <= 0.0 or p.size < 5:
        return p
    rad = int(max(1, np.ceil(3.0 * s)))
    k = np.arange(-rad, rad + 1, dtype=float)
    kern = np.exp(-0.5 * (k / s) ** 2)
    kern /= float(kern.sum())
    out = np.convolve(p, kern, mode="same")
    return _sanitize_vec(out)


def _resample(arr, m):
    """Linear resampling of a normalized vector to length m."""
    a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
    if a is None or m <= 0:
        return None
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == m:
        return a.copy()
    if n == 1:
        return np.ones(m, dtype=float)
    x_old = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)
    x_new = (np.arange(m, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(m)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = out + 1e-12
    return _sanitize_vec(out)


def _two_block_best(m):
    """Quick deterministic two-block (step) seed."""
    if m <= 8:
        return None
    best = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    for alpha in (0.25, 0.35, 0.45, 0.55, 0.65, 0.75, 0.85):
        idx = int(round(alpha * m))
        if idx <= 0 or idx >= m:
            continue
        for ratio in (0.3, 0.6, 1.0, 1.7, 2.8, 5.0):
            p = np.ones(m, dtype=float)
            p[idx:] = float(ratio)
            p = _sanitize_vec(p)
            if p is None:
                continue
            sc = _score_only(p)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best = p.copy()
    return best


def _initial_sequence(n, rng, mode=None):
    """Diverse normalized non-negative starts."""
    if n <= 0:
        return None
    if mode is None:
        mode = int(rng.integers(0, 10))
    t = (np.arange(n, dtype=float) + 0.5) / float(n)

    if mode == 0:
        z = 1.0
        for k in range(1, 10):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 1.0 / (k + 1)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z = z + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        p = np.log1p(np.exp(np.clip(z, -50.0, 50.0)))

    elif mode == 1:
        a = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 3.0))
        b = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 3.0))
        tt = np.clip(t, 1e-8, 1.0 - 1e-8)
        p = np.power(tt, a) * np.power(1.0 - tt, b)

    elif mode == 2:
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        K = int(rng.integers(1, 5))
        centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        sigmas = rng.uniform(0.02, 0.35, K)
        weights = rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0, K)
        for ii in range(K):
            c = float(centers[ii])
            sg = max(1e-6, float(sigmas[ii]))
            w = float(weights[ii])
            p = p + w * np.exp(-((t - c) / sg) ** 2)

    elif mode == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 10))
        knots = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.interp(t, knots, heights)

    elif mode == 4:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 16))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K - 1))
        blocks = np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [1.0]))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K)
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for ii in range(K):
            s = max(0, int(round(blocks[ii] * n)))
            e = max(s + 1, min(n, int(round(blocks[ii + 1] * n))))
            if e > s:
                p[s:e] = float(heights[ii])

    elif mode == 5:
        c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        k = float(rng.uniform(5.0, 30.0))
        p = 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-np.clip(k * (t - c), -50.0, 50.0)))
        p = p * np.exp(-((t - c) / 0.25) ** 2)

    elif mode == 6:
        # Asymmetric sine series.
        z = 1.0
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = float(rng.uniform(0.05, 0.95 / (k + 0.5)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z = z + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        p = z

    elif mode == 7:
        # Multi-lobe absolute sine shapes.
        alpha = float(rng.uniform(0.6, 4.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p = np.power(np.abs(np.sin(np.pi * t + ph)), alpha)
        p = p + 0.05 * np.power(np.abs(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph)), alpha)

    elif mode == 8:
        # Ramp + sine phase, good asymmetric starts.
        beta = float(rng.uniform(-0.95, 0.95))
        A = float(rng.uniform(-0.85, 0.85))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        z = 1.0 + beta * (2.0 * t - 1.0) + A * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph)
        p = np.maximum(z, 0.0)

    elif mode == 9:
        # Random multi-block with a mild sinusoidal trend.
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 12))
        edges = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, K))
        blocks = np.concatenate(([0.0], edges, [1.0]))
        heights = rng.lognormal(0.0, 1.0, K + 1)
        p = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        for ii in range(K + 1):
            s = max(0, int(round(blocks[ii] * n)))
            e = max(s + 1, min(n, int(round(blocks[ii + 1] * n))))
            if e > s:
                p[s:e] = float(heights[ii])
        trend_amp = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.2))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        p = p + trend_amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * t + ph)

    else:
        # Low-pass random noise.
        z = 1.0
        for k in range(1, 7):
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.55 / (k + 1)))
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            z = z + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * t + ph)
        p = np.exp(np.clip(z, -4.0, 4.0))

    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=float)
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.clip(p, 0.0, 1000.0)
    p = p + 1e-10
    s = float(p.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        p = np.ones(n, dtype=float)
        s = float(p.sum())
    return p / s


def _soft_weights(d, beta):
    """Softmax weights over d; if beta is tiny, uniform."""
    d = np.asarray(d, dtype=float)
    n = int(d.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    d = np.where(np.isfinite(d), d, 0.0)
    if beta <= 1e-8:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    shift = float(np.max(d))
    if not np.isfinite(shift):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=float)
    e = np.exp(beta * (d - shift))
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        idx = int(np.argmax(d))
        out = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
        out[idx] = 1.0
        return out
    return e / s


def _exact_smooth(p, beta):
    """Exact score and a smoothed max surrogate."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    m = int(p.size)
    c = _conv_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return float("inf"), float("inf")
    d = m * c
    exact = 2.0 * float(m) * maxc
    w = _soft_weights(d, beta)
    smooth = float(np.dot(w, d))
    if not np.isfinite(smooth):
        smooth = exact
    return float(exact), float(smooth)


def _gradient_metrics(p, beta):
    """Exact score, smooth surrogate, and direction h."""
    p = _sanitize_vec(p)
    if p is None:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), None, 0.0
    m = int(p.size)
    c = _conv_full(p, p)
    if c.size == 0:
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), np.array([0.0]), 0.0
    c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    maxc = float(np.max(c))
    if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
        return float("inf"), float("inf"), np.full(m, 0.0), 0.0
    d = m * c
    exact = 2.0 * float(m) * maxc
    w = _soft_weights(d, beta)
    h = _conv_valid_front(w, p)
    h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
    h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
    maxh = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0
    smooth = float(np.dot(w, d))
    if not np.isfinite(smooth):
        smooth = exact
    return float(exact), float(smooth), h, float(maxh)


def _run_mmd(p0, rng, time_limit, beta_start=30.0, beta_end=1500.0,
             alpha_init=0.2, verbose=False):
    """
    Multiplicative smoothed-max descent.  Kept as a complementary polish
    method.
    """
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0:
        sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(p)
        return p, sc

    m = int(p.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / float(m)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_exact, _ = _score_ss_fast(best)
    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, float(beta_start))

    beta = float(max(1.0, beta_start))
    beta_max = float(max(beta, beta_end))
    alpha = float(max(1e-4, alpha_init))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -10000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        cur_exact, cur_smooth, h, maxh = _gradient_metrics(p, beta)

        if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-15:
            p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, m)))
            if p_try is not None:
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                if np.isfinite(e) and e <= best_exact * 1.05:
                    p = p_try
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = e, s2
            stall += 1
        else:
            h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
            h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
            h = h / np.max(h)

            tests = []
            for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                a = alpha * float(mult)
                if 1e-4 <= a <= 20.0:
                    tests.append(float(a))
            if not tests:
                tests = [1e-4, 0.05]

            chosen = None
            ch_smooth = cur_smooth
            ch_exact = cur_exact
            ch_a = alpha

            for a in tests:
                dec = np.exp(-a * h)
                p_try = p * dec
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                s = float(p_try.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                p_try = p_try / s
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(p_try)):
                    continue
                e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                if not np.isfinite(e) or not np.isfinite(s2):
                    continue
                if s2 + 1e-11 * max(1.0, ch_smooth) < ch_smooth:
                    chosen = p_try
                    ch_smooth = s2
                    ch_exact = e
                    ch_a = a

            if chosen is not None:
                p = chosen
                cur_smooth = ch_smooth
                cur_exact = ch_exact
                alpha = ch_a
                stall = 0

                if cur_exact < best_exact:
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_exact = cur_exact
                    if verbose and (it - last_print >= 250):
                        last_print = it
                        print(f"mmd n={m} it={it} best={best_exact:.6f}")

                if cur_exact > best_exact * 1.25 and rng.random() < 0.25:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-4, alpha * 0.6)

                if stall >= 12:
                    std = 0.05 if stall < 40 else min(0.4, 0.05 + 0.005 * stall)
                    p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, m)))
                    if p_try is not None:
                        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                        p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                        e, s2 = _exact_smooth(p_try, beta)
                        if np.isfinite(e) and e <= best_exact * 1.05:
                            p = p_try
                            cur_exact, cur_smooth = e, s2
                        else:
                            p = best.copy()
                            cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
                    stall = 0

                if stall > 80:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_exact, cur_smooth = _exact_smooth(p, beta)
                    alpha = float(alpha_init)
                    beta = float(beta_start)
                    stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 20 == 0:
            beta = min(beta_max, beta * 1.15)

    return best, best_exact


def _power_mpd(p0, rng, time_limit, q_start=2.0, q_end=64.0,
               alpha_init=0.7, verbose=False):
    """
    Main multiplicative descent for a power-norm surrogate.

    At each step, given c = p*p and M=max c, use weights
        w_k = (c_k/M)^q
    and multiplicative update p <- p * exp(-alpha * h), where
        h_i = sum_j p_j w_{i+j}.

    q is annealed upward; line search is on the exact max objective,
    with sum(c^2) as a secondary flattening metric.
    """
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None:
        return None, float("inf")
    if time_limit <= 0:
        sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(p)
        return p, sc

    m = int(p.size)
    floor = 1e-18 / float(m)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score, best_ss = _score_ss_fast(best)
    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss

    q = float(max(0.5, q_start))
    q_max = float(max(q, q_end))
    alpha = float(max(1e-3, alpha_init))
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stall = 0
    it = 0
    last_print = -10000

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        c = _conv_full(p, p)
        c = np.where(np.isfinite(c), c, 0.0)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        maxc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if maxc <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(maxc):
            break

        cur_score = float(2.0 * m * maxc)
        cur_ss = float(np.dot(c, c))
        if not np.isfinite(cur_ss):
            cur_ss = 0.0

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11 or (
                abs(cur_score - best_score) <= 1e-10 and cur_ss < best_ss):
            best = p.copy()
            best_score = cur_score
            best_ss = cur_ss

        r = c / maxc
        if abs(q - 1.0) < 1e-12:
            w = r.copy()
        else:
            w = np.power(np.maximum(r, 0.0), q)
        wsum = float(np.sum(w))
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 1e-300:
            w = np.full(c.size, 1.0 / float(c.size), dtype=float)
        else:
            w = w / wsum
        # Keep a tiny uniform component so the direction does not collapse
        # to a single noisy coefficient.
        w = 0.9995 * w + 0.0005 * (1.0 / float(w.size))
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if wsum > 0.0 and np.isfinite(wsum):
            w = w / wsum

        h = _conv_valid_front(w, p)
        h = np.where(np.isfinite(h), h, 0.0)
        h = np.maximum(h, 0.0)
        maxh = float(np.max(h)) if h.size else 0.0

        if not np.isfinite(maxh) or maxh <= 1e-18:
            p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.06, m)))
            if p_try is not None:
                p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                sc_try, ss_try = _score_ss_fast(p_try)
                if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_score * 1.07, cur_score * 1.05):
                    p = p_try
                    cur_score, cur_ss = sc_try, ss_try
                else:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
            stall += 1
        else:
            h = h / maxh

            tests = []
            for mult in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
                a = alpha * float(mult)
                if 1e-3 <= a <= 50.0:
                    tests.append(float(a))
            if not tests:
                tests = [0.05, 0.2, 0.8]

            chosen = None
            chosen_score = cur_score
            chosen_ss = cur_ss
            chosen_a = alpha

            for a in tests:
                dec = np.exp(-a * h)
                cand = p * dec
                cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
                s = float(cand.sum())
                if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                    continue
                cand = cand / s
                sc, ss = _score_ss_fast(cand)
                if not np.isfinite(sc):
                    continue
                better = False
                if sc < chosen_score - 1e-11:
                    better = True
                elif abs(sc - chosen_score) <= 1e-9:
                    if ss < chosen_ss * (1.0 - 1e-8) - 1e-16:
                        better = True
                if better:
                    chosen = cand
                    chosen_score = sc
                    chosen_ss = ss
                    chosen_a = a

            if chosen is not None:
                p = chosen
                cur_score = chosen_score
                cur_ss = chosen_ss
                alpha = chosen_a
                stall = 0

                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11 or (
                        abs(cur_score - best_score) <= 1e-10 and cur_ss < best_ss):
                    best = p.copy()
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_ss = cur_ss
                    if verbose and (it - last_print >= 300):
                        last_print = it
                        print(f"power n={m} it={it} q={q:.3f} best={best_score:.6f}")

                # If the working point drifted too far, pull it back.
                if cur_score > best_score * 1.10 and rng.random() < 0.20:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
            else:
                stall += 1
                alpha = max(1e-3, alpha * 0.65)

                if stall >= 15:
                    std = 0.05 if stall < 50 else min(0.45, 0.05 + 0.004 * stall)
                    p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, std, m)))
                    if p_try is not None:
                        p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                        p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                        sc_try, ss_try = _score_ss_fast(p_try)
                        if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_score * 1.08, cur_score * 1.05):
                            p = p_try
                            cur_score, cur_ss = sc_try, ss_try
                        else:
                            p = best.copy()
                            cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss

                    # Try mild smoothing as another escape move.
                    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
                        p_smooth = _smooth_array(p, samples=1.0)
                        if p_smooth is not None:
                            sc_s, ss_s = _score_ss_fast(p_smooth)
                            if np.isfinite(sc_s) and sc_s < cur_score - 1e-10:
                                p = p_smooth
                                cur_score, cur_ss = sc_s, ss_s
                    stall = 0

                if stall > 70:
                    p = best.copy()
                    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
                    alpha = float(alpha_init)
                    q = max(1.0, q * 0.6)
                    stall = 0

        it += 1
        if it % 50 == 0:
            q = min(q_max, q * 1.04)

    return best, best_score


def _local_block_search(p0, rng, time_limit):
    """
    Exact local search by scaling random contiguous blocks.
    """
    p = _sanitize_vec(p0)
    if p is None or time_limit <= 0:
        if p is None:
            return None, float("inf")
        sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(p)
        return p, sc

    n = int(p.size)
    if n <= 2:
        sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(p)
        return p, sc

    floor = 1e-18 / float(n)
    p = np.maximum(p, floor)
    p = p / p.sum()

    best = p.copy()
    best_score, best_ss = _score_ss_fast(best)
    cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
    deadline = time.monotonic() + float(time_limit)
    stagnant = 0
    factors = (0.5, 0.7, 0.9, 1.1, 1.4, 2.0)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        r = float(rng.random())
        if r < 0.30:
            hi = max(2, n // 100 + 1)
            hi = min(n, hi)
            w = int(rng.integers(1, hi + 1))
        elif r < 0.75:
            lo = max(2, n // 500)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 50 + 1)
            hi = min(n, hi)
            w = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))
        else:
            lo = max(3, n // 50)
            hi = max(lo + 1, n // 4 + 1)
            hi = min(n, hi)
            w = int(rng.integers(lo, hi + 1))

        w = max(1, min(n, w))
        start = int(rng.integers(0, n - w + 1))

        base = p.copy()
        current_best = base
        current_score = cur_score
        current_ss = cur_ss
        changed = False

        for f in factors:
            cand = base.copy()
            cand[start:start + w] *= float(f)
            cand = np.maximum(cand, floor)
            s = float(cand.sum())
            if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
                continue
            cand = cand / s
            sc, ss = _score_ss_fast(cand)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            better = False
            if sc < current_score - 1e-11:
                better = True
            elif abs(sc - current_score) <= 1e-9:
                if ss < current_ss * (1.0 - 1e-8) - 1e-16:
                    better = True
            if better:
                current_score = sc
                current_ss = ss
                current_best = cand
                changed = True

        if changed:
            p = current_best
            cur_score = current_score
            cur_ss = current_ss
            stagnant = 0
            if cur_score < best_score - 1e-11 or (
                    abs(cur_score - best_score) <= 1e-10 and cur_ss < best_ss):
                best = p.copy()
                best_score = cur_score
                best_ss = cur_ss
        else:
            stagnant += 1
            if stagnant > 40:
                p_try = _sanitize_vec(p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n)))
                if p_try is not None:
                    p_try = np.maximum(p_try, floor)
                    p_try = p_try / p_try.sum()
                    sc_try, ss_try = _score_ss_fast(p_try)
                    if np.isfinite(sc_try) and sc_try <= max(best_score * 1.05, cur_score * 1.03):
                        p = p_try
                        cur_score, cur_ss = sc_try, ss_try
                    else:
                        p = best.copy()
                        cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
                stagnant = 0

            if stagnant > 350:
                p = best.copy()
                cur_score, cur_ss = best_score, best_ss
                stagnant = 0

    return best, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=12345, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Time-bounded search.  Returns a list of non-negative Python floats.
    """
    budget = float(budget_s)
    if not np.isfinite(budget) or budget <= 30.0:
        budget = 60.0

    start_time = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start_time + max(60.0, budget - 30.0)

    try:
        iseed = int(seed)
    except Exception:
        iseed = 12345
    rng = np.random.default_rng((iseed % 1000000007) + 1)

    cands = []
    best_arr = None
    best_score = float("inf")

    # Optional parent from previous state of the art.
    try:
        parent_raw = height_sequence_1
    except NameError:
        parent_raw = None
    except Exception:
        parent_raw = None

    parent_arr = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        try:
            parent_arr = _sanitize_vec(np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=float))
        except Exception:
            parent_arr = None
        if parent_arr is not None:
            sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(parent_arr)
            _add_candidate(cands, parent_arr, sc, 16)
            best_arr = parent_arr.copy()
            best_score = sc

    # Premely useful deterministic fallback if nothing else works.
    if best_arr is None:
        fb = _two_block_best(1024)
        if fb is None:
            fb = _initial_sequence(1024, rng, 1)
        if fb is not None:
            best_arr = _sanitize_vec(fb)
            if best_arr is not None:
                best_score, _ = _score_ss_fast(best_arr)
                _add_candidate(cands, best_arr, best_score, 16)

    # ---------- Small multi-resolution search ----------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and best_arr is not None:
        small_n = 1024
        small_starts = []

        tb = _two_block_best(small_n)
        if tb is not None:
            tb_s = _smooth_array(tb, 0.5)
            small_starts.append(tb_s if tb_s is not None else tb)

        if parent_arr is not None:
            sp = _resample(parent_arr, small_n)
            if sp is not None:
                sp_s = _smooth_array(sp, 0.5)
                if sp_s is not None:
                    small_starts.append(sp_s)

        # A deterministic-ish ramp/sine start and diverse random modes.
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 8))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 6))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 4))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 0))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 1))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 9))
        small_starts.append(_initial_sequence(small_n, rng, 7))

        scored_small = []
        for p0 in small_starts:
            if p0 is None:
                continue
            sc, ss = _score_ss_fast(p0)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored_small.append((sc, ss, p0))
        scored_small.sort(key=_start_key)

        for item in scored_small[:6]:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 70.0:
                break
            talloc = min(28.0, max(5.0, rem - 120.0))
            p, sc = _power_mpd(item[2], rng, talloc, q_start=2.0, q_end=16.0,
                               alpha_init=0.7, verbose=False)
            if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 10)
                if sc < best_score:
                    best_score = sc
                    best_arr = p.copy()

    # Choose main resolution.  4096 gives the same next-power FFT length as
    # 3072 for the full convolution, but a slightly larger search surface.
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 700.0:
        final_n = 4096
    elif rem > 300.0:
        final_n = 2048
    else:
        final_n = 1024

    # ---------- Build final-resolution starts ----------
    final_starts = []

    if rem > 120.0 and best_arr is not None:
        tb = _two_block_best(final_n)
        if tb is not None:
            tb_s = _smooth_array(tb, 1.0)
            final_starts.append(tb_s if tb_s is not None else tb)

        if parent_arr is not None:
            sp = _resample(parent_arr, final_n)
            if sp is not None:
                sp = _smooth_array(sp, 0.75)
                if sp is not None:
                    sp = _sanitize_vec(sp * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.04, final_n)))
                    if sp is not None:
                        final_starts.append(sp)

        # Resample currently best intermediate candidates.
        sc_sorted = sorted(cands, key=_cand_key)
        added_resampled = 0
        for item in sc_sorted:
            if added_resampled >= 3:
                break
            arr = item[2]
            if arr is None:
                continue
            if int(arr.size) == final_n:
                sp = arr.copy()
            else:
                sp = _resample(arr, final_n)
            if sp is not None:
                sp = _sanitize_vec(sp * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, final_n)))
                if sp is not None:
                    final_starts.append(sp)
                    added_resampled += 1

        # Resample current overall best, with a very mild noise.
        spb = _resample(best_arr, final_n)
        if spb is not None:
            spb = _sanitize_vec(spb * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.02, final_n)))
            if spb is not None:
                final_starts.append(spb)

        # Diverse structured starts; modes 8 and 6 are especially useful.
        for mode in (8, 6, 4, 0, 1, 9, 7, 2, 3, 5):
            p0 = _initial_sequence(final_n, rng, mode)
            if p0 is not None:
                final_starts.append(p0)

        scored = []
        for p0 in final_starts:
            if p0 is None:
                continue
            sc, ss = _score_ss_fast(p0)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, ss, p0))
        scored.sort(key=_start_key)

        if scored:
            sc0, _, p0 = scored[0]
            if sc0 < best_score:
                best_score = sc0
                best_arr = p0.copy()

            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            reserve = max(420.0, min(800.0, 0.30 * rem)) if rem > 700.0 else max(120.0, 0.25 * rem)
            main_budget = max(60.0, rem - reserve)
            fracs = [0.36, 0.24, 0.18, 0.14, 0.08]

            for idx, item in enumerate(scored[:6]):
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 90.0:
                    break
                frac = fracs[idx] if idx < len(fracs) else 0.08
                talloc = main_budget * float(frac)
                talloc = min(talloc, max(30.0, rem - max(60.0, reserve * 0.15)))
                if talloc < 20.0:
                    talloc = min(max(0.0, rem - 30.0), 30.0)
                if talloc <= 0.0:
                    break

                p0 = item[2]

                # Use the old smoothed-max method for one start as a
                # complementary basin, and the new power method for the rest.
                if idx == 1:
                    p, sc = _run_mmd(
                        p0,
                        rng,
                        talloc,
                        beta_start=30.0,
                        beta_end=1200.0,
                        alpha_init=0.2,
                        verbose=False
                    )
                else:
                    qst = 1.0 if idx >= 2 else 2.0
                    qen = 32.0 if idx >= 2 else 64.0
                    p, sc = _power_mpd(
                        p0,
                        rng,
                        talloc,
                        q_start=qst,
                        q_end=qen,
                        alpha_init=0.7,
                        verbose=(idx == 0)
                    )

                if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                    _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)
                    if sc < best_score:
                        best_score = sc
                        best_arr = p.copy()

    # Refresh best from pool.
    if cands:
        b_item = min(cands, key=_cand_key)
        if b_item[2] is not None and b_item[0] < best_score:
            best_score = b_item[0]
            best_arr = b_item[2].copy()

    if best_arr is None:
        best_arr = _initial_sequence(max(256, final_n // 2), rng, 1)
        best_score, _ = _score_ss_fast(best_arr)

    # ---------- Polishing ----------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 260.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t1 = min(0.35 * rem, 500.0)
        p, sc = _run_mmd(
            best_arr,
            rng,
            t1,
            beta_start=80.0,
            beta_end=2500.0,
            alpha_init=0.15,
            verbose=True
        )
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = p.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 180.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t2 = min(0.30 * rem, 380.0)
        p, sc = _power_mpd(
            best_arr,
            rng,
            t2,
            q_start=8.0,
            q_end=256.0,
            alpha_init=1.0,
            verbose=True
        )
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = p.copy()

    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 150.0 and best_arr is not None:
        t3 = min(0.30 * rem, 350.0)
        p, sc = _local_block_search(best_arr, rng, t3)
        if p is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
            _add_candidate(cands, p, sc, 16)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = p.copy()

    # ---------- Optional up-polish to longer lengths ----------
    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
    if rem > 160.0 and best_arr is not None:
        cur_len = int(best_arr.size)
        targets = []
        for mult in (2, 3, 4):
            nm = cur_len * mult
            if nm > cur_len and nm <= 12288:
                targets.append(nm)
        # Attempts dictionary-free, simple list with duplicates removed.
        unique_targets = []
        for nm in targets:
            if nm not in unique_targets:
                unique_targets.append(nm)

        for nm in unique_targets:
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 90.0:
                break
            sp = _resample(best_arr, nm)
            if sp is None:
                continue
            sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(sp)
            if not np.isfinite(sc):
                continue
            _add_candidate(cands, sp, sc, 16)
            if sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_arr = sp.copy()

            # If the resampled point is competitive, spend a short time there.
            if sc <= best_score + 0.03:
                rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 80.0:
                    tp = min(0.25 * rem, 130.0)
                    pp, scp = _power_mpd(
                        sp,
                        rng,
                        tp,
                        q_start=4.0,
                        q_end=64.0,
                        alpha_init=0.8,
                        verbose=False
                    )
                    if pp is not None and np.isfinite(scp):
                        _add_candidate(cands, pp, scp, 16)
                        if scp < best_score:
                            best_score = scp
                            best_arr = pp.copy()

                    rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
                    if rem > 50.0:
                        pl, scl = _local_block_search(
                            pp if pp is not None else sp,
                            rng,
                            min(40.0, 0.5 * rem)
                        )
                        if pl is not None and np.isfinite(scl):
                            _add_candidate(cands, pl, scl, 16)
                            if scl < best_score:
                                best_score = scl
                                best_arr = pl.copy()

    # Refresh best from pool one last time.
    if cands:
        b_item = min(cands, key=_cand_key)
        if b_item[2] is not None and b_item[0] < best_score:
            best_score = b_item[0]
            best_arr = b_item[2].copy()

    # Convert best to a plain list.
    b = _sanitize_vec(best_arr)
    if b is None:
        b = np.ones(max(256, int(final_n)), dtype=float)
    selected_list = [float(x) for x in b]

    # ---------- Optional official evaluator checks ----------
    final_cands = []
    if b is not None:
        final_cands.append(b)
    sc_sorted = sorted(cands, key=_cand_key)
    for item in sc_sorted[:6]:
        if item[2] is not None:
            final_cands.append(item[2])
    if parent_arr is not None:
        final_cands.append(parent_arr)

    evaluated = 0
    max_official = 1 if len(selected_list) > 8192 else 4

    for arr in final_cands:
        rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
        if rem < 20.0:
            break
        if evaluated >= max_official:
            break
        a = _sanitize_vec(arr)
        if a is None:
            continue
        lst = [float(x) for x in a]
        try:
            sc = float(evaluate_sequence(lst))
        except Exception:
            sc, _ = _score_ss_fast(a)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
            best_score = sc
            selected_list = lst
        evaluated += 1

    out = np.asarray(selected_list, dtype=float)
    out = np.where(np.isfinite(out), out, 0.0)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    s = float(out.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(s):
        out = np.ones(max(256, int(final_n)), dtype=float)
    else:
        out = out / s
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)

    return [float(x) for x in out]
```
